| organization.trusted.hosts.instructions | The host is expressed as a DNS name, one per line.{line_break}The wildcard `*` may be included once in a DNS name host specification. If it is included, it must be in the leftmost position, as in "*.example.com".{line_break}Use * to allow all connections. | 主机被表示为DNS名称，每行一个。{line_break}通配符 `*` 可以在DNS名称主机规范中包含一次。如果包含，它必须位于最左边的位置，例如 "*.example.com"。{line_break}使用 * 允许所有连接。 | Guidance text for trusted hosts format |

| project.type.dita.tooltip | DITA is for maps, topics, and related DITA files. With this type, you can organize publications, publish to PDF and WebHelp, review DITA quality in Problems, reuse content with keys and components, run translation jobs, search within the current map, and insert images and media from the Content library. | DITA 适用于地图、主题及相关 DITA 文件。使用此类型可以组织出版物、发布到 PDF 和 WebHelp、在问题中检查 DITA 质量、通过键和组件重用内容、运行翻译作业、在当前地图内搜索，以及从内容库插入图像和媒体。 | Shown on the DITA option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.generic | Generic | 通用 | Project type option for non-DITA content. DITA is the acronym and is not translated. |
| project.type.generic.tooltip | Generic is for projects that are not DITA. With this type, you can store any mix of files, including other XML formats such as DocBook or S1000D, Markdown, and non-XML documents. You can edit files, create review tasks, and search across the project. DITA-specific features are not available, including publications, PDF and WebHelp publishing, DITA quality checks in Problems, content reuse with keys and components, translation jobs, search within the current map, and inserting images and media from the Content library. | 通用适用于非 DITA 项目。使用此类型可以存放任意文件组合，包括其他 XML 格式（如 DocBook 或 S1000D）、Markdown 和非 XML 文档。您可以编辑文件、创建审阅任务并在整个项目中搜索。DITA 专用功能不可用，包括出版物、PDF 和 WebHelp 发布、问题中的 DITA 质量检查、通过键和组件的内容重用、翻译作业、在当前地图内搜索，以及从内容库插入图像和媒体。 | Shown on the Generic option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |

| role_change_info_none | Your platform role has been set to "No license seat". | save_new_external_resources_message | The task contains modified documents that were originally stored on a remote server, as well as new documents.\n The modified documents are opened in separate editor tabs. You need to manually save them (according to the protocol required by the remote server).\n The newly added documents must be saved locally (click "Choose directory" to save them) and then manually uploaded on the remote server. | 该任务包含最初存储在远程服务器上的已修改文档以及新文档。\n已修改的文档在单独的编辑器选项卡中打开。您需要手动保存这些文档（根据远程服务器所需的协议）。新添加的文档必须保存在本地（单击“选择目录”保存），然后手动上载到远程服务器上。 | Message presented to the user when there are new external resources are applied. |

You will get an email when that happens. | 您的更改将成为一个审查任务。项目维护者会收到通知，在其中一人接手该任务以审查并合并您的更改之前，该任务保持未分配状态。届时您将收到一封电子邮件。 | Explains, in the Submit Changes for Approval form, what happens after the collaborator submits. |

| token.endpoint.help | The token endpoint URL for the OpenID Connect provider {line_break}{line_break} For the Okta provider, replace the {okta_subdomain} in the configuration snippet below: https://{okta_subdomain}.okta.com/oauth2/v1/token {line_break}{line_break} For Microsoft Entra ID, replace {tenant_id} with the Tenant ID in the configuration snippet below: https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant_id}/oauth2/v2.0/token | OpenID Connect 提供商的令牌端点 URL {line_break}{line_break} 对于 Okta 提供商，请在以下配置片段中替换 {okta_subdomain}： https://{okta_subdomain}.okta.com/oauth2/v1/token {line_break}{line_break} 对于 Microsoft Entra ID，请在下面的配置示例中将 {tenant_id} 替换为租户 ID： https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant_id}/oauth2/v2.0/token |  |

| translation.job.log.title | Execution log | 执行日志 | Title of the dialog showing a batch job's execution log. |
| translation.job.log.unavailable | The log for this job is no longer available. | 此任务的日志已不再可用。 | Message shown in the log dialog when the execution log is no longer available (e.g. the task's workspace was cleaned up). |
| translation.job.log.waiting | Waiting for log… | 正在等待日志… | Placeholder shown in the log dialog while the first request for the log is in flight. |
